# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-09T12:11:23+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

Kwon went down in immediate pain as a run scored on the play, and was taken off the field in an ambulance. The Wiz lost the game 12-1 as the Lions clinched their first Korea Baseball Organization title in 12 years. All Rights reserved. News News I’m delighted to share the appointment of two long-tenured Planeteers into key technical leadership roles at Planet. The Planet Space Systems division is responsible for designing, building, and operating Planet Earth imaging capabilities, and these two roles will be central both to advancing our core technology roadmap and to defining the next generation of remote sensing capabilities. Back A Robotic Arm That Can Walk Across Spacecraft? Rocket Lab Built It Share this Future space missions could soon get a big leg up — or arm, rather — thanks to robotics developed by Rocket Lab in collaboration with NASA. Unlike traditional robotic arms that remain fixed in place, this version is designed to move autonomously across spacecraft and operate in zero or partial gravity, performing critical tasks like repairs, upgrades, and assembly. Rocket Lab Robotics (formerly Motiv Space Systems) developed the arm with versatility in mind. It can manipulate objects, use tools, and even perform complex tasks like repairing or upgrading space systems. Funded through NASA’s Small Business Innovation Re
  https://www.rocketlabusa.com/updates/a-robotic-arm-that-can-walk-across-spacecraft-rocket-lab-built-it/

- Sat, 07 Nov 2026 17:25:05 +0000 — 5N Plus carries the space index through a Thursday sell-off

The Copernicus Hyperspectral Imaging Mission for the Environment (CHIME) is advancing its development to be ready for launch. CHIME will provide routine hyperspectral observations from space across the visible to shortwave infrared spectral range (400-2500 nm), supporting enhanced environmental services in areas such as food security, sustainable agriculture, biodiversity monitoring, soil characterisation, mineral resources and water quality. With a swath of approximately 130 km and a spatial sampling distance of 30 m, CHIME will complement Copernicus Sentinel-2 and extend Europe's Earth observation capabilities through operational imaging spectroscopy. (NYSE: SPCE) today announced a three-year collaboration with Space for Humanity , a 501(c)(3) non-profit organization dedicated to democratizing access to space. Note that at this&nbsp;time, TraCSS accounts are only available to organizations that own and/or operate spacecraft in orbit, and National Government users .   https://space.commerce.gov/traffic-coordination-system-for-space-tracss/tracss-registration-for-spacecraft-operators/
- undated — Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification
  Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background: This test set was developed for the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and is designed to help space situational awareness (SSA) service providers test their conjunction assessment (CA) algorithms. The dataset comes with an accompanying &#8220;answer key&#8221; that lists the conjunctions in the dataset &#8211; providing a voluntary tool for self-assessment. DISCLAIMER : While this ephemeris dataset was crafted to include stressing edge cases in addition to an operational catalog, it is not completely comprehensive (as there are nearly infinite combinations of orbits and conjunction geometries possible).   https://space.commerce.gov/dataset-for-conjunction-assessment-verification/
- undated — TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies & Service Comparison
  TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies &amp; Service Comparison &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background Space debris and collision avoidance are challenges for all spacecraft, regardless of their nation of registry. As laid out in the Office of Space Commerce’s&nbsp; Vision for Global SSA Coordination , international collaboration is key to enhancing data consistency, coverage, and transparency. Both the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and the European Union Space Surveillance and Tracking (EU SST), in partnership with the commercial space situational awareness (SSA) industry, will offer free space SSA services to spacecraft operators worldwide to prevent in-orbit collisions.   https://space.commerce.gov/traffic-coordination-system-for-space-tracss/tracss-eu-sst-joint-studies-service-comparison/

- undated — NASA Unveils Moon Base Awards at MAX POWER
  NASA Unveils Moon Base Awards at MAX POWER Skip to content CAPE CANAVERAL, FL—NASA rolled out a stack of Moon Base awards Saturday during MAX POWER, ranging from new lander contracts to a recycled Mars rover.&nbsp; “Hopefully, we’re keeping industry on their toes, trying to catch up to all the solicitations we’re doing,” Moon Base Program Manager Carlos García-Galán told reporters during a press briefing. “This is what it takes to do the Moon Base in the timeline we have set forth.” Darth VADR: NASA awarded six task orders worth a combined ~$1.3B under VADR, its venture-class and rideshare launch contract. The awards represent an evolution in how NASA buys missions—adopting a buy-now, payload-later approach to reshuffle cargo as hardware matures. “Under the current CLPS contract, it’s all or nothing, which might be fine if you’re only doing one or two of those per year,” NASA Administrat
  https://payloadspace.com/nasa-unveils-moon-base-awards-at-max-power/
- undated — AST SpaceMobile To Build New Blacklift Rocket
  AST SpaceMobile To Build New Blacklift Rocket Skip to content Amid a growing launch crunch, AST SpaceMobile ($ASTS) is building its own ride to space. On Friday, the company announced it is building Blacklift, a 20-ton rocket designed to launch its BlueBird D2D satcom constellation to LEO.&nbsp; “The Blacklift rocket is the springboard to significantly expand our total addressable market, providing AST SpaceMobile with regular unconstrained access to orbit and providing for most favorable owners’ economics in the industry,” Abel Avellan, AST SpaceMobile’s founder, chairman, and CEO said in a statement.&nbsp; Rocket details: Blackliftis targeting a first flight in late 2028—an incredibly aggressive timeline to get a new launch vehicle up and running.   https://payloadspace.com/ast-spacemobile-to-build-new-blacklift-rocket/

  European Bodyguard Sats are Closer Than You Think Skip to content Last week, we covered Europe’s newfound efforts to fund greater defensive—and offensive —capabilities in orbit. This week, we’re starting to get a sense of when those capabilities might be ready to fly. Spoiler alert: It’s sooner than you probably think. On Wednesday, Swiss propulsion startup PAVE Space announced its plans to launch a demo mission of its IBEX spacecraft—designed as a highly maneuverable platform to host commercial and defense payloads—as early as February 2028.&nbsp; The mission will pave the way (pun intended) for future missions to conduct on-orbit “reconnaissance, co-orbital denial—acting like a bodyguard—jamming, and capture or neutralization of a hostile spacecraft,” according to the announcement.   How to Expand Reliable, Flexible Access to Space - SpaceNews Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content Home / How to Expand Reliable, Flexible Access to Space Get your Icon Awards tickets before the rate goes up on Nov. 11. Join us in Washington, D.C. on Dec. 1. Secure your spot. The space industry launched more rockets into orbit last year than ever before, yet satellite operators are confronting an unexpected problem: There still aren’t enough rides to go around. And increasingly, the shortage isn’t simply about the number of rockets. Satellite companies want launches to more orbital destinations, with more regularity and aboard a wider range of vehicles. Instead, much of the industry has come to depend on a relatively small number of frequently launched rockets that often go to similar orbits.   Former NASA Chief Economist is New Head of Canadian Space Agency &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com Alexander MacDonald is the new President of the Canadian Space Agency. Canadian-born MacDonald was NASA&#8217;s Chief Economist until December 2024. Alex MacDonald (from LinkedIn ). The Canadian Government announced MacDonald&#8217;s selection this evening. He succeeds Lisa Campbell, who had held the position since 2020. Her five-year term was extended by one year. A dual Canadian-U.S. citizen born in Canada, MacDonald was NASA&#8217;s first Chief Economist after serving in several other positions at the agency. As Chief Economist, he was part of NASA&#8217;s Office of Technology, Policy, and Strategy, which was eliminated by DOGE last year although MacDonald left the agency a few months earlier. March 16, 2026 – Ottawa – National Defence / Canadian Armed Forces Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. Today in Ottawa, the Honourable David J. McGuinty, Minister of National Defence, announced an historic $200 million investment in core infrastructure for a Canadian-owned spaceport, along with other major new space-related capabilities and initiatives. The orbital outpost was soaring 267 miles above the South Atlantic Ocean at the time of this photograph. Credit: CSA Canadian Space Agency (CSA) astronaut Joshua Kutryk and NASA&#8217;s Anil Menon installed the International Space Station&#8217;s eighth and final new solar array on Nov. 5, finishing an upgrade to the station&#8217;s power system that began in 2021. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht

Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . - undated — Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna
  Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna &#8211; MEDIA INAF Chiunque abbia provato a usare uno smartphone lasciato per ore al freddo sa che batterie e basse temperature non vanno particolarmente d’accordo. Sulla Luna, però, il problema è decisamente più serio: al polo sud, durante la notte, le temperature possono scendere fino a circa 200 gradi sotto zero, mentre nelle zone illuminate dal Sole la superficie può superare i 100 gradi Celsius. E se in futuro astronauti, rover e strumenti scientifici dovranno trascorrere sempre più tempo da quelle parti, qualcosa dovrà pur tenerli accesi. È il problema al centro di SpearS, acronimo di Solid Power for Extreme Environment Adaptability and Resilience in Space , un progetto della Nasa appena partito sotto la guida di Donald Dornbusch , del Glenn Research Center di Cleveland, per sviluppare batterie allo stato solido capaci di resistere alle
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/10/19/come-si-tiene-accesa-una-batteria-sulla-luna/

#### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

Publié le 21 octobre 2026 à 10:31 Et si l’avenir de l’aviation légère passait par cet ULM électrique ?&nbsp;© Revolt'Air Ajoutez Futura à vos sources préférées Suivez-nous sur Discover Suivez-nous sur Google Actualités Rêver de toucher le ciel et d’atteindre l’espace n’a rien d’impossible, surtout en embrassant des études qui permettent d’y parvenir. #### Austria in Space (national space platform) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php
- undated — Über Austro-Satellit PEGASUS : Der Erdbeobachtungssatellit PEGASUS feierte Juni 2021 bereits seinen 4. Geburtstag.
  Über Austro-Satellit PEGASUS - Austria in Space Über Austro-Satellit PEGASUS Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Der Erdbeobachtungssatellit PEGASUS feierte Juni 2021 bereits seinen 4. Geburtstag. Pegasus ist jetzt bereits vier Jahre aktiv. (Perihelion ) PEGASUS ist Teil des Satellitenprojekts QB50 und wurde 2017 von Indien aus in die Umlaufbahn gebracht. Eines der Hauptziele des Projekts QB50 war es, Studierenden auf der ganzen Welt die Möglichkeit zu geben, einen Satelliten zu entwickeln und zu betreiben. Das Ergebnis dieses Projekts in Österreich war PEGASUS, der von Studierenden der Fachhochschule Wiener Neustadt, der Technischen Universität Wien und der Universität Wien in Zusammenarbeit mit dem Österreichischen Weltraumforum entwickelt wurde. Der Satellit ist noch immer im Einsatz und überträgt weiterhin Daten. Projekt: QB50, ein Netzwerk von 36 Nanosatellit
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/happybirthdaypegasus.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

- undated — Space Cowboys
  ## [De midterms en de dreigende lanceercrisis SpaceX-hegemonie, sterren in alle kleuren, telescoop SWIFT is naar beneden gekomen, Chinezen gaan naar de maan en leuke nieuwe ruimtevaartboeken voor op de verlanglijstjes. Dat en meer bespreken Philippe Schoonejans, Luc van den Abeelen en Thijs Roes in deze nieuwe Space Cowboys. Schrijf je in voor de nieuwsbrief van Space Cowbows en krijg elke aflevering in je mailbox zodra deze uitkomt - op thysroes.nl/spacecowboys Space Cowboys behandelt ruimtevaart- en astronomienieuws van land, planeet en daarbuiten. Afwisselend gepresenteerd door: Thijs Roes, Herbert Blankesteijn, Michel van Baal, Luc van den Abeelen, Philippe Schoonejans, Erik Laan, Marieke Baan, Jeffrey Bout, Inge Loes ten Kate, André Kuipers, Bastiaan Bom, Nadine Duursma, Marco Langbroek, Bruno van Wayenburg, Charlotte Pouwels, Marc Heemskerk en Nick Poelstra. Space Cowboys is te vinden op https://www.linkedin.com/company/space-cowboys-podcast/ https://x.com/spacecowboyspod https://mastodon.social/@SpaceCowboys@mastodon.nl De hosts mailen? Dat kan via spacecowboyspod@gmail.com Nieuwsbrief Space Cowboys: https://thysroes.nl/spacecowboys/nieuwsbrief Links voor deze aflevering: Mi
  https://www.bnr.nl/podcast/spacecowboys

- undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/

  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha

- undated — How airspace closures affect aviation - and more
  How airspace closures affect aviation - and more Companies category powered by How airspace closures affect aviation - and more 12:44 - 09 November 2026 Companies # Flight Mode Geopolitical tensions are now affecting the skies as well. When airspace is closed or considered unsafe, airlines are forced to change course, travelling more kilometres to reach the same destination. The consequences do not end with the duration of a flight. More kilometres mean more fuel, higher operating costs, and greater environmental impact. What a closed airspace means Airspace can be closed or restricted for many reasons. Armed conflicts, military exercises, threats from missiles or drones, political tensions, air traffic control strikes, and natural disasters or severe weather can all lead to flights being banned or significantly restricted. It is also important to clarify that a closure does not always m

  Die Technik, um riesige Datenmengen per Laser über große Distanzen zu übertragen, wurde in Deutschland entwickelt. Genutzt wird sie jetzt primär von den USA. Experten fürchten, dass das Know-how ins Ausland abwandert.

  Die Entdeckung des ExoMars Trace Gas Orbiters in der Nähe eines alten Vulkans könnte bedeuten, dass unter der Oberfläche Magma brodelt. Das wäre bedeutsam für die Suche nach Leben auf dem Mars.

Monaco gagne du terrain dans un domaine en pleine expansion : l’activité aérospatiale privée. Le 12 octobre dernier, le Gouvernement Princier a annoncé la création du Bureau des affaires spatiales, conformément à la volonté du Prince Albert II de diversifier l’économie et de renforcer l’attractivité de la Principauté. - undated — Kalendar Jutro koje je promijenilo Zagreb
  Jutro koje je promijenilo Zagreb - Index.hr Preskoči na glavni sadržaj 9. studenoga 1880. Index kalendar Tekst se nastavlja ispod oglasa U RANO jutro 9. studenoga 1880. godine Zagreb je pogodio jedan od najrazornijih potresa u njegovoj povijesti. Snažno podrhtavanje tla izazvalo je paniku među stanovnicima, teško oštetilo brojne zgrade i ostavilo dubok trag u razvoju grada. No katastrofa je imala i neočekivanu posljedicu, pokrenula je veliku obnovu tijekom koje je Zagreb postupno dobivao izgled moderne europske metropole. Potres se dogodio u 7 sati, 33 minute i 52 sekunde, kada je većina Zagrepčana započinjala svoj uobičajeni dan. Epicentar se nalazio na području Medvednice, a magnituda potresa procjenjuje se na oko 6,3 prema Richterovoj ljestvici. Podrhtavanje je trajalo svega nekoliko sekundi, ali posljedice su bile goleme. Dimnjaci su padali s krovova, zidovi su pucali, a s pročelja z

Bojový pilot Aleš Svoboda uspěl v náročném výběrovém řízení Evropské kosmické agentury, kam se hlásilo víc než 22 tisíc lidí z Evropy. Svoboda je astronautem v záloze a teď mu svitla reálná naděje, že se do vesmíru skutečně podívá v rámci některé z misí Axiom. Jeho cestu podporuje vláda i premiér Petr Fiala. Jiří Hadač 9. listopadu 2026 0:10 9. 11. 2026 Žádné komentáře 0 6 min čtení Při nedávném letu Starship se na oběžnou dráhu poprvé dostaly družice Starlink třetí generace. Na jejich příchod se přitom čekalo několik let. SpaceX původně plánovalo velké družice druhé generace, které měly využívat obrovský nákladový prostor nové rakety. Jenže vývoj Starship se protáhl a&nbsp;firma proto musela připravit menší variantu V2 Mini, kterou bylo možné vynášet pomocí Falconu 9. Nyní se situace konečně obrátila a&nbsp;na oběžnou dráhu se dostaly družice, pro které byla Starship od začátku určena. Podíváme se proto, jak se V3 liší od předchozích generací a&nbsp;co jejich nástup znamená pro budoucnost Starlinku

Týmy nedávno dokončily instalaci solárních panelů na sluneční štít sondy, což je konstrukce navržená především k&nbsp;odstínění slunečního záření. Štít bude udržovat infračervený vesmírný dalekohled v&nbsp;chladu během vyhledávání blízkozemních asteroidů a&nbsp;komet a&nbsp;zároveň bude sondě dodávat elektrickou energii. Konstrukce slunečního štítu je vysoká 6 metrů a&nbsp;u&nbsp;základny měří 2,4 metru na šířku. Její dva ploché panely, spojené pod úhlem 23,5 stupně, byly na začátku letošního roku pokryty 864 vzájemně propojenými solárními čl

Jejím hlavním cílem bylo komplexní mapování povrchu Marsu a&nbsp;studium jeho atmosféry, topografie, mineralogického složení a&nbsp;magnetického pole. Sonda odstartovala 7.&nbsp;listopadu 1996 na raketě Delta II z&nbsp;rampy LC-17A na floridském mysu Canaveral a&nbsp;na oběžné dráze Marsu pracovala přibližně devět let, přestože svou primární misi dokončila už v&nbsp;roce 2001. Data nasbíraná sondou pomohla například při výběru přistávací oblasti roverů Spirit i&nbsp;Opportunity a&nbsp;sonda samotná později zajišťovala retranslaci dat pro komunikaci s&nbsp;oběma vozítky.   Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu National Mourning It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. - undated — The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION]
  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu National Mourning Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. It was at that point that the Baltic Sea began to be described, in simplified media shorthand, as a so-called »NATO inland sea«. For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them.   Razpis Evropske vesoljske agencije namenjen slovenskim podjetjem in institucijam | GOV.SI Domov Novice Razpis Evropske vesoljske agencije namenjen slovenskim podjetjem in institucijam 2. 10. 2026 Ministrstvo za gospodarstvo, delo in šport Evropska vesoljska agencija (angleško European Space Agency - ESA) je objavila drugi razpis odkar je Slovenija polnopravna članica ESA za slovenska podjetja in institucije (angleško Requesting Party Activity - RPA) za zbiranje okvirnih predlogov projektov izbranih tipov aktivnosti z namenom krepitve vesoljskega sektorja v Sloveniji. RPA razpis je namenjen samo podjetjem in akademskim ter raziskovalnim organizacijam s sedežem v Sloveniji. Namen razpisa je krepitev slovenskega vesoljskega sektorja in spodbujanje vključitve obstoječih ter novih podjetij in organizacij, ki še niso aktivna na področju vesoljskih tehnologij. Norge har vært en romnasjon siden Andøya rakettskytefelt skjøt opp den første forskningsraketten i 1962. Siden har vi bygget opp rominfrastruktur tilpasset landets behov til en hver tid. Vårt medlemskap i ESA og samarbeid med EU har fått stor betydning for dette. Etter småsatellittenes inntog har Norge også bygget opp en flåte av egne nasjonale satellitter som skal dekke behov som vi ikke får dekket gjennom internasjonale samabeid.   Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Til hovedinnhold Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Bærebjelken i norsk satsing på romvirksomhet er deltakelse i internasjonalt samarbeid. I første rekke gjennom den europeiske romorganisasjonen ESA og EUs romprogram. I tillegg har Norge samarbeidsavtaler med enkeltland. Norge og ESA Den europeiske romorganisasjonen (ESA) er en internasjonal forsknings- og utviklingsorganisasjon grunnlagt i 1975. ESAs formål er å fremme samarbeid mellom europeiske stater innen romforskning og romteknologi for vitenskapelige formål og for utvikling av operasjonsdyktige romanvendelsessystemer. Samarbeid med ESA ESA er den Europeiske romorganisasjonen, og gjennom delegatvirksomheten administrerer Romsenteret Norges deltagelse i ESA. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. Kuwait University has signed an agreement with the Kuwait Foundation for the Advancement of Sciences (KFAS) to establish and operate the Kuwait National Space Research Center. The signing took place in the presence of Kuwait University President, Prof. Dina Musaed Al-Mailem, and KFAS Director General, Dr. Ameenah Rajab Farhan, alongside senior officials from both institutions. By&nbsp; Patrick Alushula Business Reporter Nation Media Group Thank you for reading businessdailyafrica.com Show plans Five insurers have teamed up for a geothermal exploration risk cover that will compensate investors up to $2 million (Sh257.5 million) when they drill and fail to find sufficient output in Kenya, in a move aimed at spurring private investments in the green energy space. ICEA Lion, Mayfair Insurance, Old Mutual, GA Insurance and Kenya Re as a consortium will underwrite the risks with the hope that the cover will provide incentive to potential investors and banks to back such projects.   ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3. Establishment of the Agency (1)There is hereby established a state corporation to be known as the Kenya Space Agency.(2)The Agency shall be a body corporate with perpetual succession and a common seal and shall, in its corporate name, be capable of—(a)suing and being sued;(b)taking, purchasing or otherwise acquiring, holding, charging or disposing of movable and immovable property;(c)receiving, investing, borrowing and lending money; and(d)doing or performing any such other thing or act, including entering into such contracts as may be
  https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

- undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection.   https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

- undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ

#### ISRO (government, en, newsroom)

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De

Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. - undated — NASA 砸百億美元啟動 CLPS 2 計畫，SpaceX、藍色起源等八家業者搶建月球基地
  NASA 砸百億美元啟動 CLPS 2 計畫，SpaceX、藍色起源等八家業者搶建月球基地 | TechNews 科技新報 美國國家航空暨太空總署（NASA）7 日在佛州甘迺迪太空中心宣布一系列月球合約，核心是總額上限 100 億美元、為期十年的「商業月球酬載服務」第二期（CLPS 2），為月球南極永久基地鋪路。 CLPS 2獲選的八家業者包括SpaceX、藍色起源（Blue Origin）、螢火蟲航太（Firefly Aerospace）、直覺機器（Intuitive Machines）、ispace美國公司、洛克希德馬丁、諾斯洛普格魯曼與航海家科技（Voyager Technologies），未來將競標運送科學儀器與基礎設施至月球南極的任務。NASA強調，100億美元是訂單上限，並非已撥付款項。現行CLPS合約上限也從26億美元調高至42億美元，支應基地初期建設。 NASA署長艾薩克曼（Jared Isaacman）表示，月球基地將逐次任務打造，太空人重返月球時，首批基地組件已在月面等候。 藍色起源另取得9,400萬美元基本合約，以「藍月一號」著陸器運送NASA核動力探測車PROMISE至月球南極，另有1.4億美元績效選擇權。這輛六輪探測車將探查可能蘊藏水冰的區域，由噴射推進實驗室（JPL）利用好奇號與毅力號火星車的備用零件打造以壓低成本，預定2028年底發射，任務為期18個月。 運輸方面，Arrow Science and Technology、藍色起源與Impulse Space共獲六筆總值約13億美元的任務訂單，測試地月貨運導航與偵察著陸區的機器人無人機；SpaceX則2028年以星艦部署地月通訊衛星。Honeybee Robotics、Redwire與Yank Technologies也獲選打造結合太陽能與無線輸電的月球電力暨通訊塔。 NASA awards billions in contracts to expand lunar exploration, build moon base NASA Awards Moon Base Transportati
  https://technews.tw/2026/11/09/nasa-awards-billions-in-contracts-to-expand-lunar-exploration-build-moon-base/

#### Korea Times (national_press, en, feed)

- Mon, 09 Nov 2026 12:02:03 GMT — Wiz shortstop diagnosed with orbital fracture after being struck by batted ball in Korean Series game
  The KT Wiz announced Monday their shortstop Kwon Dong-jin will not require surgery after suffering an orbital fracture during a Korean Series game the previous day. Kwon took a hard grounder off the bat of Samsung Lions hitter Kim Young-woong in the bottom of the eighth inning of Game 5 of the series at Daegu Samsung Lions Park in the southeastern city of Daegu on Sunday. Kwon went down in immediate pain as a run scored on the play, and was taken off the field in an ambulance. The Wiz lost the game 12-1 as the Lions clinched their first Korea Baseball Organization title in 12 years. According to the Wiz, further tests done on Monday showed an orbital fracture but Kwon had sustained no damage to his eyes, meaning he will not need surgery. The club said doctors will monitor Kwon's condition
  https://www.koreatimes.co.kr/sports/20261109/wiz-shortstop-diagnosed-with-orbital-fracture-after-being-struck-by-batted-ball-in-korean-series-game?utm_source=rss
- Mon, 09 Nov 2026 12:02:03 GMT — Prison terms sought for ex-security officials for leaking THAAD-related intelligence
  The prosecution on Monday sought prison terms for three officials who served under former President Moon Jae-in on charges of leaking intelligence related to the THAAD U.S. missile defense system. Prosecutors requested 10-month prison sentences for Chung Eui-yong, former national security adviser, and Jeong Kyeong-doo, former defense minister, during their trial on power abuse charges at the Seoul Central District Court. They also sought a two-year prison term for Suh Choo-suk, former first deputy national security adviser. Chung and Jeong are accused of giving orders to inform activists opposed to South Korea's hosting of the Terminal High Altitude Area Defense (THAAD) system of operations to replace THAAD battery missiles and radar equipment in Seongju, 215 kilometers southeast of Seoul,
